<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://motorolanews.com/motorola-three-new-b2b-solutions-at-mwc-2026/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Motorola announces a partnership with GrapheneOS Foundation</span><span class='zh'>摩托罗拉宣布与 GrapheneOS 基金会达成合作</span></a><span class="dom">motorolanews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Motorola teams up with the GrapheneOS Foundation to focus on security-hardened Android foundations and open-source collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>摩托罗拉与 GrapheneOS 基金会达成合作，表明在 Android 安全加固与开源协作方面的重点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Motorola teams up with the GrapheneOS Foundation to focus on security-hardened Android foundations and open-source collaboration. The move could influence future device security updates, transparency, and baseline protections across Motorola hardware.</span><span class='zh'>摩托罗拉与 GrapheneOS 基金会达成合作，表明在 Android 安全加固与开源协作方面的重点。此举可能影响未来的安全更新、透明度以及硬件基础安全特性的建立。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A court ruling that FLOCK camera footage must become public records prompts authorities to shut down the network, highlighting tensions between surveillance, privacy, and accountability.</span><span class='zh'>法院裁定 FLOCK 监控录像应作为公开记录，促使相关部门关闭网络，凸显监控、隐私与问责之间的紧张关系。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A court ruling that FLOCK camera footage must become public records prompts authorities to shut down the network, highlighting tensions between surveillance, privacy, and accountability. The move underscores legal risk for city surveillance programs.</span><span class='zh'>法院裁定 FLOCK 监控录像应作为公开记录，促使相关部门关闭网络，凸显监控、隐私与问责之间的紧张关系。此举凸显城市级监控项目的法律风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="http://karpathy.github.io/2026/02/12/microgpt/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microgpt</span><span class='zh'>MicroGPT（微型GPT）</span></a><span class="dom">karpathy.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Karpathy unveils MicroGPT, a compact, self-contained GPT-inspired framework designed to run with minimal dependencies.</span><span class='zh'>Karpathy 介紹了 MicroGPT——一個極簡、可在低依賴環境下運行的 GPT 類框架，適合在中等硬件上試驗與本地推理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Karpathy unveils MicroGPT, a compact, self-contained GPT-inspired framework designed to run with minimal dependencies. It targets experimentation on modest hardware and on-device reasoning, enabling offline AI tooling and edge applications. This approach lowers the barrier to LM research beyond cloud deployments.</span><span class='zh'>Karpathy 介紹了 MicroGPT——一個極簡、可在低依賴環境下運行的 GPT 類框架，適合在中等硬件上試驗與本地推理。它促進離線 AI 工具與邊緣應用的發展，讓 LM 研究不再僅限於雲端。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mandel-macaque/memento" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If AI writes code, should the session be part of the commit?</span><span class='zh'>如果 AI 写代码，会话应成为提交的一部分吗？</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>If AI-assisted sessions influence code changes, teams may consider embedding session provenance in commits or metadata for traceability.</span><span class='zh'>若 AI 辅助的编辑会影响代码变更，团队可考虑在提交中嵌入会话出处或元数据以实现可追溯性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>If AI-assisted sessions influence code changes, teams may consider embedding session provenance in commits or metadata for traceability. This raises questions about reproducibility, review, and auditing of AI-influenced work.</span><span class='zh'>若 AI 辅助的编辑会影响代码变更，团队可考虑在提交中嵌入会话出处或元数据以实现可追溯性。这引发关于可复现性、审查与对 AI 影响的审计等问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ejholmes.github.io/2026/02/28/mcp-is-dead-long-live-the-cli.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>When does MCP make sense vs CLI?</span><span class='zh'>什么时候 MCP 比 CLI 更合适</span></a><span class="dom">ejholmes.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A comparison of when a higher-level command protocol (MCP) provides meaningful abstraction and automation over a plain CLI, and when the simplicity and direct control of a CLI wins.</span><span class='zh'>讨论在何种场景下更高层的命令协议 MCP 能带来对比简单 CLI 更有意义的抽象与自动化，以及何时应偏好 CLI 的简单性与直接控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A comparison of when a higher-level command protocol (MCP) provides meaningful abstraction and automation over a plain CLI, and when the simplicity and direct control of a CLI wins. It emphasizes tradeoffs like readability, scripting, and ecosystem maturity.</span><span class='zh'>讨论在何种场景下更高层的命令协议 MCP 能带来对比简单 CLI 更有意义的抽象与自动化，以及何时应偏好 CLI 的简单性与直接控制。强调可读性、脚本化与生态成熟度等权衡。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">15</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.ivanturkovic.com/2026/02/25/ai-made-writing-code-easier-engineering-harder/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI Made Writing Code Easier. It Made Being an Engineer Harder</span><span class='zh'>AI 让写代码更容易，却让工程师更难</span></a><span class="dom">ivanturkovic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI reduces the burden of writing code but shifts engineering work toward maintaining, testing, and integrating AI-generated artifacts.</span><span class='zh'>人工智能降低了编写代码的门槛，但将工程工作的重点转向维护、测试与集成 AI 产物。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI reduces the burden of writing code but shifts engineering work toward maintaining, testing, and integrating AI-generated artifacts. It raises expectations and reshapes skills and team workflows in nontrivial ways.</span><span class='zh'>人工智能降低了编写代码的门槛，但将工程工作的重点转向维护、测试与集成 AI 产物。它提高了期望值，改变了技能需求和团队的工作方式。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nested decision rules in decision trees can express complex logic with interpretability, but risk overfitting and brittleness if not managed carefully.</span><span class='zh'>嵌套的决策规则使决策树能够表达复杂逻辑并保持可解释性，但若处理不当也易过拟合和脆弱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nested decision rules in decision trees can express complex logic with interpretability, but risk overfitting and brittleness if not managed carefully. Properly structured, they enable powerful, rule-based reasoning in ML systems.</span><span class='zh'>嵌套的决策规则使决策树能够表达复杂逻辑并保持可解释性，但若处理不当也易过拟合和脆弱。通过恰当的结构设计，可以让基于规则的推理在 ML 系统中发挥强大作用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://maurycyz.com/misc/c_files/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why does C have the best file API</span><span class='zh'>为什么 C 语言拥有最好的文件 API</span></a><span class="dom">maurycyz.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>C&#x27;s file I/O API is praised for its low-level control and performance, offering precise management of buffers and system calls that higher-level languages abstract away.</span><span class='zh'>C 语言的文件 I/O API 因其底层控制与性能而备受赞誉，能够对缓冲区与系统调用进行精细管理，这些在高级语言中往往被封装。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>C&#x27;s file I/O API is praised for its low-level control and performance, offering precise management of buffers and system calls that higher-level languages abstract away.</span><span class='zh'>C 语言的文件 I/O API 因其底层控制与性能而备受赞誉，能够对缓冲区与系统调用进行精细管理，这些在高级语言中往往被封装。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://venturebeat.com/technology/alibabas-new-open-source-qwen3-5-medium-models-offer-sonnet-4-5-performance" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Qwen3.5 122B and 35B models offer Sonnet 4.5 performance on local computers</span><span class='zh'>Qwen3.5 122B 与 35B 模型在本地电脑上达到 Sonnet 4.5 性能</span></a><span class="dom">venturebeat.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Alibaba&#x27;s Qwen3.5 family offers 122B and 35B variants that match Sonnet 4.5 performance on local hardware, signaling strong open-source on-device LLM capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>阿里巴巴的 Qwen3.5 家族提供 122B 与 35B 版本，在本地硬件上达到 Sonnet 4.5 的性能水平，显示開源大模型在本地推理方面的強大能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Alibaba&#x27;s Qwen3.5 family offers 122B and 35B variants that match Sonnet 4.5 performance on local hardware, signaling strong open-source on-device LLM capabilities. This narrows the gap to cloud services and fuels offline AI exploration, though licensing and ecosystem support remain considerations.</span><span class='zh'>阿里巴巴的 Qwen3.5 家族提供 122B 与 35B 版本，在本地硬件上达到 Sonnet 4.5 的性能水平，显示開源大模型在本地推理方面的強大能力。這縮小了與雲端服務的差距，促進離線 AI 的探索，但授權與生態系統支援仍需留意。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.rockoder.com/beyondthecode/cognitive-debt-when-velocity-exceeds-comprehension/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cognitive Debt: When Velocity Exceeds Comprehension</span><span class='zh'>認知債務：當速度超過理解力</span></a><span class="dom">rockoder.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that pushing development velocity too hard creates cognitive debt—hidden mental load and architectural debt that slows future work.</span><span class='zh'>文章認為過度追求開發速度會產生認知債務，即藏匿的心理負擔與架構負擔，最終拖慢未來的工作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that pushing development velocity too hard creates cognitive debt—hidden mental load and architectural debt that slows future work. It promotes better onboarding, documentation, and mental models to keep velocity sustainable. The takeaway: speed must be matched with clarity.</span><span class='zh'>文章認為過度追求開發速度會產生認知債務，即藏匿的心理負擔與架構負擔，最終拖慢未來的工作。建議透過更好的上手指引、文件與心智模型，讓速度持續可行。核心觀點是：速度需要與清晰度並行。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Examines how Windows 95 UI decisions were tested and validated, illustrating early usability engineering practices and their impact on modern design.</span><span class='zh'>回顾 Windows 95 的用户界面设计与可用性工程方法，揭示早期 UX 实践及其对现代设计的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Examines how Windows 95 UI decisions were tested and validated, illustrating early usability engineering practices and their impact on modern design. The article distills lessons on consistency, feedback, and discoverability that still influence UX today.</span><span class='zh'>回顾 Windows 95 的用户界面设计与可用性工程方法，揭示早期 UX 实践及其对现代设计的影响。文中提炼出一致性、反馈与易发现性的经验，至今仍影响用户体验。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An exploration of the reliability and safety risks of autonomous AI agents, showing why you shouldn&#x27;t over-rely on them for critical tasks. The piece advocates robust architectures, verification, and tamper-resistance to prevent misbehavior or exploitation.</span><span class='zh'>探讨自治 AI 代理的可靠性与安全风险，解释为何不应过度依赖它们完成关键任务。文章主张构建更强健的架构、可验证性与防篡改设计，以防止误操作或被利用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tomwojcik.com/posts/2026-02-15/finding-the-right-amount-of-ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What AI coding costs you</span><span class='zh'>AI 编码给你带来的成本</span></a><span class="dom">tomwojcik.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Quantifies the hidden costs of AI-assisted coding, including subscriptions, debugging overhead, context-switch costs, and long-tail maintenance.</span><span class='zh'>量化 AI 辅助编码的隐性成本，包括订阅、调试开销、上下文切换成本与长期维护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Quantifies the hidden costs of AI-assisted coding, including subscriptions, debugging overhead, context-switch costs, and long-tail maintenance. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://deadlime.hu/en/2026/02/22/computer-generated-dream-world/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Computer-generated dream world: Virtual reality for a 286 processor</span><span class='zh'>286 处理器上的计算机生成梦境世界：虚拟现实</span></a><span class="dom">deadlime.hu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A playful exploration of virtual reality running on an 80286-class processor demonstrates how constrained hardware can still deliver immersive experiences.</span><span class='zh'>在 80286 级别的处理器上实现虚拟现实，展示了受限硬件仍能提供沉浸式体验的可能性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A playful exploration of virtual reality running on an 80286-class processor demonstrates how constrained hardware can still deliver immersive experiences. It&#x27;s a reminder that the bottlenecks of old tech can inspire new approaches to efficiency and performance.</span><span class='zh'>在 80286 级别的处理器上实现虚拟现实，展示了受限硬件仍能提供沉浸式体验的可能性。通过一系列渲染与输入的巧妙技巧，作品把复古硬件推向极限。它提醒人们，旧技术的瓶颈也能激发更高效的设计思路。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260226-how-ai-can-read-your-thoughts" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Evolving descriptive text of mental content from human brain activity</span><span class='zh'>从脑部活动中生成描述性文本</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers continue to evolve methods that translate brain signals into descriptive text, inching toward more natural and expressive brain-computer interfaces.</span><span class='zh'>研究者正在改进将脑信号转化为描述性文本的方法，向更自然、富表达的脑机接口迈进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers continue to evolve methods that translate brain signals into descriptive text, inching toward more natural and expressive brain-computer interfaces. The advances enable richer glimpses into mental content while raising questions about privacy, consent, and how we measure &#x27;thought&#x27; in real time. These developments broaden potential uses from communication aids to neurotech monitoring.</span><span class='zh'>研究者正在改进将脑信号转化为描述性文本的方法，向更自然、富表达的脑机接口迈进。相关进展使对思维内容的理解更丰富，同时引发隐私、知情同意以及如何实时衡量“思想”的讨论。这些发展拓展了从信息传递帮助到神经技术监测的潜在应用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ellanew.com/2026/03/02/ptpl-197-record-retrieve-from-a-personal-knowledgebase" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How to record and retrieve anything you&#x27;ve ever had to look up twice</span><span class='zh'>记录并检索你曾多次查找过的任何信息</span></a><span class="dom">ellanew.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A personal knowledge-base approach that captures repeated lookups into a searchable archive, making it easy to retrieve past queries without re-remembering details.</span><span class='zh'>通过把重复查找的信息纳入可搜索的个人知识库，帮助用户轻松检索历史查询，避免重复记忆。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A personal knowledge-base approach that captures repeated lookups into a searchable archive, making it easy to retrieve past queries without re-remembering details. It emphasizes ownership and organization of your own search history, enabling faster discovery and reducing cognitive load. This could inform tools and workflows for knowledge workers.</span><span class='zh'>通过把重复查找的信息纳入可搜索的个人知识库，帮助用户轻松检索历史查询，避免重复记忆。该方法强调对自身搜索历史的拥有和组织，提升检索效率、降低认知负担。它还可能为知识工作者的工作流提供新思路。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/sama/status/2027578652477821175" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI agrees with Dept. of War to deploy models in their classified network</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 与战争部在其机密网络中部署模型达成协议</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI reaches an agreement to deploy its models inside the War Department&#x27;s classified network, marking a rare government-use approval for advanced AI.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 达成在战争部机密网络中部署其模型的协议，标志着在高敏环境中对先进 AI 的罕见应用获批。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI reaches an agreement to deploy its models inside the War Department&#x27;s classified network, marking a rare government-use approval for advanced AI. The deal raises questions about security, vendor oversight, and the scale of AI integration into sensitive environments. It signals a potential path for enterprise and defense collaborations, with tight governance requirements.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 达成在战争部机密网络中部署其模型的协议，标志着在高敏环境中对先进 AI 的罕见应用获批。该交易引发对安全、供应商监管以及在敏感场景中部署 AI 的规模等问题的讨论。它预示着企业与防务领域日后更紧密的协作之路，同时将伴随严格的治理要求。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A blistering take aimed at a flawed system, outlining why it fails users and how it could be replaced.</span><span class='zh'>这是一篇对一个存在缺陷的系统的愤怒式评论，阐述其为何让用户失望以及可以如何被替换。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A blistering take aimed at a flawed system, outlining why it fails users and how it could be replaced. The author argues for radical change and accountability, using blunt rhetoric to cut through bureaucracy. The piece serves as a rallying cry for redesigning or delegitimizing broken processes.</span><span class='zh'>这是一篇对一个存在缺陷的系统的愤怒式评论，阐述其为何让用户失望以及可以如何被替换。作者主张进行根本性变革并追究责任，用直言不讳的语言穿透官僚体制。文章像是对被破坏流程的重新设计与正名的号召。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://joelsiks.com/posts/cpp-emergency-pool-72kb-allocation/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why is the first C++ (m)allocation always 72 KB?</span><span class='zh'>为什么第一次的 C++ 内存分配总是 72 KB？</span></a><span class="dom">joelsiks.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Exploring the initial memory allocation in C++ reveals allocator design, page alignment, and arena behavior.</span><span class='zh'>探究 C++ 首次内存分配为何常见为 72 KB，揭示分配器设计、页面对齐与内存区的行为。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Exploring the initial memory allocation in C++ reveals allocator design, page alignment, and arena behavior. The observation matters because the first allocation can influence startup latency and fragmentation, shaping optimization choices for allocators and data layout.</span><span class='zh'>探究 C++ 首次内存分配为何常见为 72 KB，揭示分配器设计、页面对齐与内存区的行为。此现象会影响启动延迟与碎片化，从而引导开发者在分配器与内存布局上的优化选择。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cosmiciron/vmprint" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I built a zero-browser, pure-JS typesetting engine for bit-perfect PDFs</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：零浏览器、纯 JavaScript 的位精确排版引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A pure-JS, zero-dependency typesetting engine that outputs bit-perfect PDFs without relying on a browser&#x27;s layout engine.</span><span class='zh'>一个纯 JavaScript、零依赖的排版引擎，输出位精确的 PDF，不依赖浏览器排版引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A pure-JS, zero-dependency typesetting engine that outputs bit-perfect PDFs without relying on a browser&#x27;s layout engine. It demonstrates how much you can push in the JS sandbox and opens up offline, deterministic printing workflows. This could inform reproducible publishing pipelines and minimal runtime footprints.</span><span class='zh'>一个纯 JavaScript、零依赖的排版引擎，输出位精确的 PDF，不依赖浏览器排版引擎。展示了在纯 JS 沙箱内还能实现的极致排版能力，并为离线、确定性打印工作流打开了可能性。这样的实现可能推动可重复出版流程和最小化运行时开销的探索。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://unsloth.ai/docs/basics/unsloth-dynamic-2.0-ggufs" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Unsloth Dynamic 2.0 GGUFs</span><span class='zh'>Unsloth Dynamic 2.0 GGUFs</span></a><span class="dom">unsloth.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Unsloth Dynamic 2.0 introduces an updated GGUF format that supports dynamic loading and on-demand quantization for large language model weights.</span><span class='zh'>Unsloth Dynamic 2.0 引入更新的 GGUF 格式，支持权重的动态加载和按需量化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Unsloth Dynamic 2.0 introduces an updated GGUF format that supports dynamic loading and on-demand quantization for large language model weights. The update improves loading speed and memory efficiency on consumer hardware, enabling larger models to run with fewer GPUs. This matters for developers and researchers building on-device AI tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Unsloth Dynamic 2.0 引入更新的 GGUF 格式，支持权重的动态加载和按需量化。此改动提升了在消费级硬件上的加载速度与内存利用效率，使更大模型在更少显卡上也能运行。对在设备端构建 AI 工具链的开发者和研究者尤为相关。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">68</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.timcappalli.me/p/passkeys-prf-warning/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Don&#x27;t use passkeys for encrypting user data</span><span class='zh'>不要用 Passkeys 对用户数据进行加密</span></a><span class="dom">blog.timcappalli.me</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Passkeys are designed for authentication, not as encryption keys.</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 旨在用于身份验证，而非加密密钥。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Passkeys are designed for authentication, not as encryption keys. Using them to encrypt data complicates key management, revocation, and sharing, creating security and compliance risks. The piece advocates using proper encryption keys or a KMS instead of reusing authentication material for data confidentiality.</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 旨在用于身份验证，而非加密密钥。将其用于数据加密会使密钥管理、撤销与共享变得复杂，带来安全与合规风险。应使用专门的加密密钥或密钥管理服务来保护数据机密性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers show Rydberg atoms can detect clear signals from a handheld radio, enabling compact, sensitive radio sensing.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员证明 Rydberg 原子能够从手持无线电中检测到清晰信号，带来紧凑高灵敏的射频传感能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers show Rydberg atoms can detect clear signals from a handheld radio, enabling compact, sensitive radio sensing. This opens possibilities for lightweight receivers and new quantum-enabled sensing applications.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员证明 Rydberg 原子能够从手持无线电中检测到清晰信号，带来紧凑高灵敏的射频传感能力。此技术有望推动轻量接收设备与量子感测应用的发展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/3624725" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Science of Detecting LLM-Generated Text (2024)</span><span class='zh'>识别 LLM 生成文本的科学研究</span></a><span class="dom">dl.acm.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers survey the methods for detecting text produced by large language models, highlighting the limitations of current classifiers and watermarking approaches.</span><span class='zh'>研究评估了现有检测大语言模型生成文本的方法，指出当前分类器和水印等手段的局限性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers survey the methods for detecting text produced by large language models, highlighting the limitations of current classifiers and watermarking approaches. The study shows detectors struggle with newer model generations and domain shift, underscoring that no single method is reliable across all contexts. The takeaway is that robust detection will require a multi-pronged strategy combining statistical cues, watermarking, and human-in-the-loop validation.</span><span class='zh'>研究评估了现有检测大语言模型生成文本的方法，指出当前分类器和水印等手段的局限性。对最新模型和跨领域文本的检测仍存在显著挑战，单一方法难以全面可靠。关键在于结合统计线索、水印技术以及人工复核的多方位策略来提升鲁棒性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cyloq.se/en/research/cve-2026-0714-tpm-sniffing-luks-keys-on-an-embedded-device" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TPM-Sniffing LUKS Keys on an Embedded Linux Device [CVE-2026-0714]</span><span class='zh'>在嵌入式 Linux 设备上嗅探 LUKS 密钥的 TPM 漏洞（CVE-2026-0714）</span></a><span class="dom">cyloq.se</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A vulnerability allows TPM to leak LUKS passphrases on certain embedded devices, enabling offline decryption under conditions.</span><span class='zh'>一个漏洞允许 TPM 在某些嵌入式设备上泄露 LUKS 密码短语，条件成立时可进行离线解密。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A vulnerability allows TPM to leak LUKS passphrases on certain embedded devices, enabling offline decryption under conditions. Exploits require trusted hardware, but firmware updates and mitigations are essential for affected devices. The bug highlights the fragility of hardware-backed encryption when firmware is compromised.</span><span class='zh'>一个漏洞允许 TPM 在某些嵌入式设备上泄露 LUKS 密码短语，条件成立时可进行离线解密。攻击需要可信硬件，但固件更新和缓解措施对受影响设备至关重要。这一漏洞凸显了在固件被攻破时，硬件背后的加密也可能脆弱。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ad-si/Woxi" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Woxi: Wolfram Mathematica Reimplementation in Rust</span><span class='zh'>Woxi：用 Rust 重实现 Wolfram Mathematica</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Woxi is a Rust-based reimplementation of Wolfram Mathematica, aiming to deliver high-performance symbolic computation.</span><span class='zh'>Woxi 用 Rust 重实现 Wolfram Mathematica，目标是提供高性能的符号计算工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Woxi is a Rust-based reimplementation of Wolfram Mathematica, aiming to deliver high-performance symbolic computation. It exemplifies efforts to democratize advanced math tooling while highlighting the challenges of reimplementing a mature system.</span><span class='zh'>Woxi 用 Rust 重实现 Wolfram Mathematica，目标是提供高性能的符号计算工具。该项目展示了让高端数学工具更易得的努力，同时也揭示了重实现一个成熟系统的挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.vox.com/climate/480083/beef-agriculture-deforestation-amazon-rainforest" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Foods destroying rainforests, in one simple chart</span><span class='zh'>导致雨林破坏的食品，一张简单图表</span></a><span class="dom">vox.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A simple chart links a small set of foods—beef, soy, palm oil, and others—to rainforest deforestation, outlining the supply-chain drivers and environmental impact.</span><span class='zh'>一张简单图表将少数食品（如牛肉、大豆、棕榈油等）与雨林砍伐联系起来，梳理供给链驱动因素及环境影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A simple chart links a small set of foods—beef, soy, palm oil, and others—to rainforest deforestation, outlining the supply-chain drivers and environmental impact. The piece emphasizes how consumer choices and production systems drive ecological harm and calls for sustainable sourcing.</span><span class='zh'>一张简单图表将少数食品（如牛肉、大豆、棕榈油等）与雨林砍伐联系起来，梳理供给链驱动因素及环境影响。文章强调消费选择与生产体系对生态破坏的推动作用，并呼吁更可持续的采购。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.ivanturkovic.com/2026/01/22/history-software-simplification-cobol-ai-hype/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Eternal Promise: A History of Attempts to Eliminate Programmers</span><span class='zh'>永恒的承诺：试图消灭程序员的历史</span></a><span class="dom">ivanturkovic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Software has long promised to automate away developers, and this history shows why that promise repeatedly fails.</span><span class='zh'>软件领域长期被承诺要自动化淘汰程序员，但这一承诺屡屡难以实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Software has long promised to automate away developers, and this history shows why that promise repeatedly fails. The piece surveys milestones from early auto-generation to modern AI-assisted tooling, arguing that complexity and creative problem-solving keep programmers indispensable.</span><span class='zh'>软件领域长期被承诺要自动化淘汰程序员，但这一承诺屡屡难以实现。本文梳理了从早期自动生成代码到现代 AI 辅助工具的里程碑，指出复杂性和人类解决问题的能力使程序员不可替代。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://joelchrono.xyz/blog/setting-up-phones-is-a-nightmare/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Setting up phones is a nightmare</span><span class='zh'>设置新手机是一场噩梦</span></a><span class="dom">joelchrono.xyz</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Onboarding modern smartphones is often painful, with data migrations, sign-ins, and ecosystem lock-in slowing setup.</span><span class='zh'>现代智能手机的初始设置常常让人倍感痛苦，数据迁移、账户登录和生态体系绑定等环节拖慢了整个设置过程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Onboarding modern smartphones is often painful, with data migrations, sign-ins, and ecosystem lock-in slowing setup. The post catalogs friction points and suggests pragmatic improvements—better data portability, unified sign-in, and clearer transfer tooling. Reducing friction here could improve satisfaction for device makers and carriers.</span><span class='zh'>现代智能手机的初始设置常常让人倍感痛苦，数据迁移、账户登录和生态体系绑定等环节拖慢了整个设置过程。文章列出主要痛点，并建议改进方向，如更好的数据可移植性、统一登录与更清晰的迁移工具。减少这些摩擦有望提升用户满意度并惠及设备制造商与运营商。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Talking to strangers isn’t just social nicety—it&#x27;s a skill with real payoff in work and life.</span><span class='zh'>与陌生人交谈不仅是一种社交礼仪，而是一项在工作与生活中都能带来实际收益的技能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.quantamagazine.org/the-man-who-stole-infinity-20260225/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>New evidence that Cantor plagiarized Dedekind?</span><span class='zh'>新证据指向康托尔抄袭了德德金？</span></a><span class="dom">quantamagazine.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New evidence suggests Cantor may have drawn on Dedekind’s work, reigniting debate over priority in the foundations of set theory.</span><span class='zh'>新证据指向康托尔可能借鉴了德德金的工作，再度点燃关于集合论基础中的优先权之辩。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New evidence suggests Cantor may have drawn on Dedekind’s work, reigniting debate over priority in the foundations of set theory. The discussion reframes historical claims about originality and the evolution of infinity concepts.</span><span class='zh'>新证据指向康托尔可能借鉴了德德金的工作，再度点燃关于集合论基础中的优先权之辩。讨论重新构建了关于原创性与无穷概念演进的历史主张。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.morling.dev/blog/hardwood-new-parser-for-apache-parquet/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hardwood: A New Parser for Apache Parquet</span><span class='zh'>Hardwood：Apache Parquet 的全新解析器</span></a><span class="dom">morling.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hardwood introduces a new parser for Apache Parquet designed to improve parsing speed and resilience relative to existing implementations.</span><span class='zh'>Hardwood 为 Apache Parquet 提供了一种全新的解析器，目标是提升解析速度与容错能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hardwood introduces a new parser for Apache Parquet designed to improve parsing speed and resilience relative to existing implementations. It focuses on robust handling of the Parquet metadata and nested schemas, with potential for streaming reads and better ergonomics for developers integrating Parquet data into pipelines. This could influence data tooling performance in analytics stacks that rely on Parquet.</span><span class='zh'>Hardwood 为 Apache Parquet 提供了一种全新的解析器，目标是提升解析速度与容错能力。它强调对 Parquet 元数据和嵌套模式的稳健处理，并可能支持流式读取，方便将 Parquet 数据接入数据管线。此举可能提升分析栈中 Parquet 相关工具的性能与可用性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://vondra.me/posts/the-real-cost-of-random-io/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The real cost of random I/O</span><span class='zh'>随机 I/O 的真实成本</span></a><span class="dom">vondra.me</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Random I/O incurs far higher latency than sequential access due to seeks and cache behavior.</span><span class='zh'>随机 I/O 相比顺序访问带来更高的延迟，原因在于寻道和缓存行为。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Random I/O incurs far higher latency than sequential access due to seeks and cache behavior. The analysis shows how random patterns translate into higher CPU and I/O costs, influencing database design and storage expectations.</span><span class='zh'>随机 I/O 相比顺序访问带来更高的延迟，原因在于寻道和缓存行为。分析显示随机模式如何转化为更高的 CPU 与 I/O 成本，影响数据库设计与存储预期。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>As spacecraft become data-intensive, current communication networks struggle with bandwidth, latency, and reliability.</span><span class='zh'>随着航天器数据密集化，现有通信网络在带宽、时延和可靠性方面面临挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>As spacecraft become data-intensive, current communication networks struggle with bandwidth, latency, and reliability. The piece argues for redesigned space communication architectures and edge processing to meet mission demands.</span><span class='zh'>随着航天器数据密集化，现有通信网络在带宽、时延和可靠性方面面临挑战。文章主张对航天通信体系进行重新设计，并强化边缘处理以满足任务需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.16398" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Chorba: A novel CRC32 implementation (2024)</span><span class='zh'>Chorba：一种新颖的 CRC32 实现（2024）</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Chorba introduces a novel CRC32 implementation that aims to boost throughput and reduce memory usage.</span><span class='zh'>Chorba 提出了一种新颖的 CRC32 实现，目标是提升吞吐量并降低内存占用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Chorba introduces a novel CRC32 implementation that aims to boost throughput and reduce memory usage. The approach may employ table-free algorithms or processor-specific optimizations to speed up checksums on large data streams. Efficient CRC implementations matter for data integrity in storage, networking, and embedded systems.</span><span class='zh'>Chorba 提出了一种新颖的 CRC32 实现，目标是提升吞吐量并降低内存占用。该方法可能采用无表算法或专用处理器优化，以在大数据流中加速校验和计算。高效的 CRC 实现对存储、网络和嵌入式系统的数据完整性至关重要。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-10114-5" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41586-026-10114-5/MediaObjects/41586_2026_10114_Fig1_HTML.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='nature.com' data-c='#10b981' data-tag='Science'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">101</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-10114-5" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sub-second volumetric 3D printing by synthesis of holographic light fields</span><span class='zh'>亚秒级体积三维打印：通过全息光场合成实现</span></a><span class="dom">nature.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate sub-second volumetric 3D printing by synthesizing holographic light fields that encode an entire object in a single exposure.</span><span class='zh'>研究通过全息光场的合成来实现亚秒级的体积打印，将整个三维对象在单次曝光中编码到光场中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate sub-second volumetric 3D printing by synthesizing holographic light fields that encode an entire object in a single exposure. By projecting a carefully designed holographic light field, the resin cures in three dimensions without layer-by-layer scanning, enabling ultra-fast fabrication of complex geometries. The method hinges on precise light-field control and rapid image synthesis hardware.</span><span class='zh'>研究通过全息光场的合成来实现亚秒级的体积打印，将整个三维对象在单次曝光中编码到光场中。避免传统逐层打印的过程，利用高精度光场控制实现复杂几何体的超快速制造。该技术若实现规模化的材料和硬件兼容性，可能彻底改变快速原型制作与按需制造的速度与灵活性。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">102</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://evanhahn.com/introducing-gzpeek/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gzpeek: Tool to Parse Gzip Metadata</span><span class='zh'>Gzpeek：解析 Gzip 元数据的工具</span></a><span class="dom">evanhahn.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gzpeek exposes metadata stored in gzip files, such as header flags, modification times, and optional fields that aren’t always visible in standard tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Gzpeek 能揭示 gzip 文件中的元数据，如头部标志、修改时间以及常规工具较少显示的可选字段，适用于取证、数据校验和调试流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gzpeek exposes metadata stored in gzip files, such as header flags, modification times, and optional fields that aren’t always visible in standard tooling. This makes it valuable for forensics, data verification, and debugging pipelines. The tool helps engineers understand data provenance and integrity more precisely.</span><span class='zh'>Gzpeek 能揭示 gzip 文件中的元数据，如头部标志、修改时间以及常规工具较少显示的可选字段，适用于取证、数据校验和调试流程。该工具帮助工程师更精确地理解数据来源与完整性。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">103</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.popsci.com/technology/floppy-disk-archivist-project/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The archivist preserving decaying floppy disks</span><span class='zh'>守护逐渐腐化的软盘的档案管理员</span></a><span class="dom">popsci.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A look at the archival effort to salvage data from aging floppy disks reveals the fragility of early digital media and the ingenuity required to preserve it.</span><span class='zh'>聚焦拯救逐渐腐朽的软盘数据的档案工作，揭示早期数字媒体的脆弱性以及保护它所需的巧思。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A look at the archival effort to salvage data from aging floppy disks reveals the fragility of early digital media and the ingenuity required to preserve it. Archivists pair hardware quirks with emulation and meticulous metadata to recover manuscripts, photos, and software from decades past.</span><span class='zh'>聚焦拯救逐渐腐朽的软盘数据的档案工作，揭示早期数字媒体的脆弱性以及保护它所需的巧思。档案人员将硬件差异与仿真技术、细致的元数据结合，恢复数十年前的文稿、照片与软件。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Frankensqlite is a Rust reimplementation of SQLite that supports concurrent writers, offering a different concurrency model from the original C-based engine.</span><span class='zh'>Frankensqlite 是一个用 Rust 重实现的 SQLite，支持并发写入，展现了 Rust 在嵌入式数据库中的安全性优势。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Frankensqlite is a Rust reimplementation of SQLite that supports concurrent writers, offering a different concurrency model from the original C-based engine. It showcases Rust&#x27;s safety guarantees in embedded databases and raises questions about compatibility, tooling, and performance trade-offs.</span><span class='zh'>Frankensqlite 是一个用 Rust 重实现的 SQLite，支持并发写入，展现了 Rust 在嵌入式数据库中的安全性优势。它带来与原生 SQLite 的兼容性、工具链和性能取舍相关的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://smallcultfollowing.com/babysteps/blog/2026/02/27/dada-internal-references/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How Dada enables internal references</span><span class='zh'>Dada 如何实现内部引用</span></a><span class="dom">smallcultfollowing.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Dada provides a mechanism for internal references within a document, codebase, or data structure, enabling cross-links without external bindings.</span><span class='zh'>Dada 提供在文档、代码库或数据结构中创建内部引用的机制，使跨引用无需外部绑定成为可能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Dada provides a mechanism for internal references within a document, codebase, or data structure, enabling cross-links without external bindings. The post explains the design, usage patterns, and benefits for navigation, refactors, and consistency. It also discusses potential pitfalls and compatibility considerations.</span><span class='zh'>Dada 提供在文档、代码库或数据结构中创建内部引用的机制，使跨引用无需外部绑定成为可能。文章介绍了设计思路、使用模式与对导航、重构及一致性的重要性，同时讨论潜在的陷阱与兼容性考量。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A pragmatic guide to enabling CORS on a blog, outlining how to configure response headers to allow cross-origin asset access while avoiding common pitfalls that may introduce security risks.</span><span class='zh'>本文提供在博客上启用 CORS 的实用指南，讲解如何配置响应头以允许跨域资源访问，同时避免常见的安全隐患。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A pragmatic guide to enabling CORS on a blog, outlining how to configure response headers to allow cross-origin asset access while avoiding common pitfalls that may introduce security risks. Includes tips on when to enable, what to expose, and how to audit the impact.</span><span class='zh'>本文提供在博客上启用 CORS 的实用指南，讲解如何配置响应头以允许跨域资源访问，同时避免常见的安全隐患。包括何时开启、暴露哪些信息以及如何评估影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://spiraldb.com/post/cascading-compression-with-btrblocks" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Have your cake and decompress it too</span><span class='zh'>同时实现高压缩与快速解压的级联压缩方法</span></a><span class="dom">spiraldb.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cascading compression technique demonstrates how to chain multiple compression layers to achieve small on-disk size while preserving fast random access.</span><span class='zh'>级联压缩方法展示了将多种压缩层级串联起来，在保持快速随机访问的同时实现更小的磁盘占用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cascading compression technique demonstrates how to chain multiple compression layers to achieve small on-disk size while preserving fast random access. The approach is relevant for large datasets, backups, and systems where storage and latency both matter.</span><span class='zh'>级联压缩方法展示了将多种压缩层级串联起来，在保持快速随机访问的同时实现更小的磁盘占用。该思路对大数据集、备份和需要兼顾存储与延迟的系统具有实际意义。</span></p>
